+# 📋 চেঞ্জলগ: 3123003
+
+## কমিট তথ্য
+- **হ্যাশ:** 3123003
+- **লেখক:** SupremeAI CI Bot <ci-bot@supremeai.dev>
+- **সময়:** 2026-07-03T12:26:44.409879
+- **বার্তা:** fix: override pytest addopts in subprocess to fix roundtrip coverage test
+
+- The roundtrip test runs pytest in a subprocess with -p no:pytest_cov
+- But pyproject.toml addopts still include --cov=core which fails without the plugin
+- Added --override-ini=addopts= to clear coverage args in subprocess
+- Fixes: AssertionError in test_existing_gcp_roundtrip_coverage
+
+## ফাইল পরিবর্তন
+```
+ backend/tests/test_firebase_integration.py | 1 +
+ 1 file changed, 1 insertion(+)
+
+```
+
+## ডিটেইল পার্থক্য
+
+```diff
+diff --git a/backend/tests/test_firebase_integration.py b/backend/tests/test_firebase_integration.py
+index 73e24e7..af948b2 100644
+--- a/backend/tests/test_firebase_integration.py
++++ b/backend/tests/test_firebase_integration.py
+@@ -131,6 +131,7 @@ def test_existing_gcp_roundtrip_coverage():
+             "pytest",
+             "-p",
+             "no:pytest_cov",
++            "--override-ini=addopts=",
+             f"{test_path_prefix}/test_gcp_integration.py::test_gcp_firestore_integration_queue",
+             f"{test_path_prefix}/test_gcp_integration.py::test_gcp_pubsub_publish_pull",
+             f"{test_path_prefix}/test_gcp_integration.py::test_gcp_cloud_run_router_route",
+
+```
+
+---
+*এই রিপোর্ট স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে তৈরি করা হয়েছে*
